						<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-bf1da01 elementor-widget elementor-widget-heading" data-id="bf1da01" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="heading.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
					<h2 class="elementor-heading-title elementor-size-default">Velkommen til
Danske Mediers Nyhedsbrev</h2>				</div>
				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-83c37c7 elementor-widget elementor-widget-text-editor" data-id="83c37c7" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="text-editor.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
									<p><strong>Modtag AKTUELT fra Danske Medier</strong></p><p>Danske Mediers nyhedsbrev giver dig aktuelt indhold fra- og viden om mediebranchen. Samtidig indeholder nyhedsbrevene holdninger og nyt fra Danske Medier – herunder en fast digital klumme og information om events og kurser. </p><p>Nyhedsbrevet AKTUELT udkommer hver fredag i ulige uger, undtagen i ferieperioder.</p>								</div>
				</div>
				<div class="elementor-element elementor-element-dc51a1a elementor-widget elementor-widget-text-editor" data-id="dc51a1a" data-element_type="widget" data-e-type="widget" data-widget_type="text-editor.default">
				<div class="elementor-widget-container">
									<p>* <span class="s1">V</span>ed tilmelding til nyhedsb<span class="s2">rev</span>et samtyk<span class="s2">k</span>er du til, at Dans<span class="s2">k</span>e Medier må sende dig de <span class="s2">v</span>algte nyhedsb<span class="s2">rev</span>e via e-mail. Du kan til enh<span class="s2">v</span>er tid træk<span class="s2">k</span>e samtyk<span class="s2">k</span>et tilbage. Afmelding kan s<span class="s2">k</span>e <span class="s2">v</span>ed at klik<span class="s2">k</span>e på lin<span class="s2">k</span>et i bunden af nyhedsb<span class="s2">rev</span>ene eller skri<span class="s2">v</span>e til  <a href="&#109;&#x61;i&#108;&#x74;o&#58;&#x6d;a&#x69;&#x6c;&#64;&#x64;a&#110;&#x73;k&#101;&#x6d;e&#x64;&#x69;&#101;&#x72;&#x2e;&#100;&#x6b;">mail@dans<span class="s2">k</span>emedie<span class="s3">r</span>.dk</a>. Du kan <a href="https://danskemedier.dk/cookie-og-privatlivspolitik/">her læse me<span class="s2">r</span>e om, h<span class="s2">v</span>o<span class="s2">r</span>dan vi håndte<span class="s2">r</span>er dine personoplysninge<span class="s3">r</span></a>.</p>								</div>
				</div>
					</div>
		</div>

					<script>
				( () => {
					const lazyloadRunObserver = () => {
						const lazyloadBackgrounds = document.querySelectorAll( `.e-con.e-parent:not(.e-lazyloaded)` );
						const lazyloadBackgroundObserver = new IntersectionObserver( ( entries ) => {
							entries.forEach( ( entry ) => {
								if ( entry.isIntersecting ) {
									let lazyloadBackground = entry.target;
									if( lazyloadBackground ) {
										lazyloadBackground.classList.add( 'e-lazyloaded' );
									}
									lazyloadBackgroundObserver.unobserve( entry.target );
								}
							});
						}, { rootMargin: '200px 0px 200px 0px' } );
						lazyloadBackgrounds.forEach( ( lazyloadBackground ) => {
							lazyloadBackgroundObserver.observe( lazyloadBackground );
						} );
					};
					const events = [
						'DOMContentLoaded',
						'elementor/lazyload/observe',
					];
					events.forEach( ( event ) => {
						document.addEventListener( event, lazyloadRunObserver );
					} );
				} )();
			</script>

    (() => {
        const html = document.getElementsByTagName('html')[0];
        const region = "east"; 
        let isApp = false;
        
        if (region === 'fyn') {
            isApp = navigator.userAgent === 'tv2fyn-tv2reg-app-median'

            isApp = navigator.userAgent.indexOf('median') !== -1

        } else {

        }
    })();

<script>

(() => {

  const waitForCookieNotice = ({ timeoutMs = 20000, intervalMs = 100 } = {}) => {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
      if (window.CookieNotice) {
        resolve(window.CookieNotice);
        return;
      }

      const startedAt = Date.now();
      const timer = setInterval(() => {
        if (window.CookieNotice) {
          clearInterval(timer);
          resolve(window.CookieNotice);
          return;
        }

        if (Date.now() - startedAt >= timeoutMs) {
          clearInterval(timer);
          reject(`PostHog: window.CookieNotice not defined after ${timeoutMs}ms. Not loading PostHog.`);
        }
      }, intervalMs);
    });
  };

  const hasStatisticsConsent = waitForCookieNotice({ timeoutMs: 20000, intervalMs: 200 }).then(() => {
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      if (window.CookieNotice.cookieExists('tv2reg_cookie_consent')) {
        const json = window.CookieNotice.getCookie('tv2reg_cookie_consent');
        const obj = JSON.parse(json);
        if (obj && obj.consent && Array.isArray(obj.consent)) {
          const statisticsConsent = obj.consent.find((e) => e.handle === 'statistics');
          if (statisticsConsent && statisticsConsent.value) {
            resolve();
          } else {
            console.warn('PostHog: Statistics cookie consent not given. Not loading PostHog:', obj);
          }
        } else {
          console.warn('PostHog: Statistics cookie malformed or not found. Not loading PostHog:', obj);
        }
      } else {
        window.CookieNotice.on('accepted', (group) => {
          if (group === 'statistics') {
            resolve();
          }
        });
      }
    });
  });

  hasStatisticsConsent.then(() => {

<!-- CookieConsent Script -->
<script>
    var cbCookies = document.cookie.split('; ');
    var cbConsentCookieEntry = null;
    var cbMarketingConsent = false; // Default to false
    var cbStatisticsConsent = false; // Default to false
    for (var cbI = 0; cbI < cbCookies.length; cbI++) {
        if (cbCookies[cbI].indexOf('tv2reg_cookie_consent=') === 0) {
            cbConsentCookieEntry = cbCookies[cbI];
            break;
        }
    }
    if (cbConsentCookieEntry && cbConsentCookieEntry.indexOf('=') !== -1) {
        var cbConsentParts = cbConsentCookieEntry.split('=');
        var cbConsentCookieValue = cbConsentParts.length > 1 ? decodeURIComponent(cbConsentParts[1]) : null;
        if (cbConsentCookieValue) {
            try {
                var cbConsentData = JSON.parse(cbConsentCookieValue);
                for (var cbJ = 0; cbJ < cbConsentData.consent.length; cbJ++) {
                    var consentItem = cbConsentData.consent[cbJ];
                    if (consentItem.handle === 'marketing') {
                        cbMarketingConsent = consentItem.value;
                    } else if (consentItem.handle === 'statistics') {
                        cbStatisticsConsent = consentItem.value;
                    }
                }
            } catch (cbE) {
                // console.error('Error parsing consent cookie:', cbE);
            }
        }
    }

    // Now you can use cbMarketingConsent and cbStatisticsConsent variables as needed
</script>
